# Agents · D · part 19

1,004 entries, designer to dev. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **designer**: Produces visual mockups for stories with UI components. Proposes 3 options per story — foundational mocks (sequential) explore visual DNA across all three options; detail mocks (parallel) reuse the locked foundational shell verbatim and explore content-area layout/density variations across the three options. Outputs HTML + screenshots into the epic's mocks/ directory. ([djpate/kryptonite](https://github.com/djpate/kryptonite/blob/HEAD/skills/kryptonite/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: UI/UX 設計師。讀 docs/00-prd.md，寫 docs/00-ui-spec.md，並用 #REF 連回功能錨點。 沒有前端時標註純後端並略過 UI。Use when a greenfield PRD exists and the user needs screens, interaction states, or an explicit no-UI note. ([donlee612/ai-dev-libraries](https://github.com/donlee612/ai-dev-libraries/blob/HEAD/ai-greenfield-starter/.cursor/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: Architecture and interface designer: compares options, studies how flagship projects solve the same problem, and writes a decision with trade-offs. Use when a design decision is needed before planning. ([DonQuinleone/Dotfiles](https://github.com/DonQuinleone/Dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.local/share/claude/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: Designs an app, site or dashboard from a brief, as a tappable prototype that reads as native on iPhone, iPad, Mac or Android, and serves it as a live preview while it builds. Use when someone wants a design made rather than a rule looked up ("design me an app for…", "make the iPad version", "redesign this screen"). Run a whole session as it with `claude --agent onplatform:designer`. ([doppe7gangar/onplatform](https://github.com/doppe7gangar/onplatform/blob/HEAD/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: Transform game jam briefs into detailed Game Design Documents. Use when starting a jam to create the GDD from the brief. ([dpid/jam-master](https://github.com/dpid/jam-master/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/designer.md))
- **designer**: Builder role for UI and UX cards: designs and implements interfaces in the card's git worktree, checks them with browser screenshots, opens a pull request and requests review. Dispatched by the kanban daemon, not invoked by hand. ([DresvyanskiyDenis/claude-kanban-plugin](https://github.com/DresvyanskiyDenis/claude-kanban-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/kanban-claude/agents/designer.md))

- **designer**: The design/architecture specialist. - **desktop**: - **Electron owns the stable desktop origin**: the renderer always loads `https://desktop.internal.hubris.build/`, never raw loopback URLs. In dev, Electron proxies frontend asset requests to Vite and backend requests to the Rust dev server. In packaged mode, Electron serves bundled `apps/web/dist` files itself and proxies `/api` and `/_hubris` to the packaged Rust runtime on an ephemeral loopback ([jimeh/hubris](https://github.com/jimeh/hubris/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/desktop.md))

- **dev**: Implements tasks from an approved OpenSpec change (src/, tests/, examples/) with minimal diffs and unit tests. Use to delegate coding from openspec/changes/&lt;name&gt;/tasks.md. Never edits openspec artifacts, never commits. ([hamc/blastproof](https://github.com/hamc/blastproof/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/dev.md) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **dev**: Implements features based on a development plan, and fixes bugs reported by the test agent ([zjio26/forge](https://github.com/zjio26/forge/blob/HEAD/agents/dev.md) · ★11)
- **dev**: Developer agent for auto-dev. Implements tasks autonomously with TDD discipline, ADR compliance, and skill-guided patterns. ([fernandobandeira/slopbusters](https://github.com/fernandobandeira/slopbusters/blob/HEAD/archive/opencode/agents/dev.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
